# MEH-1171 — Manual Testing → Automated Suite: Triage Matrix

> **STATUS: 🔒 DRAFT — WAIT GATE. Awaiting Sapir's approval of this matrix (including STALE deletions) before any conversion work starts.** Per ADR-016 amendment (MEH-1155), the DO-NOT-MERGE marker on the PR overrides tier auto-merge authority.
>
> Ticket A scope: classification only — **zero app fixes**. Suspected app bugs found during triage are logged in § Findings below as input for Ticket B.

## Phase 0 report

**Source doc:** `docs/MANUAL_TESTING.md` @ `origin/staging` (2,295 lines at triage start; +1 section — "map-quality batch PR 1", 6 items — landed mid-triage via the eb7ff655 staging merge and is classified in the last 6 rows).

| Stat | Value |
|---|---|
| `##` sections | 120 (+140 `###` subsections) |
| Checklist items (`- [ ]`) | 1,060 |
| Matrix rows | 1,074 (1,060 items + 14 section-level rows for checkbox-less sections) |

**Existing automation inventory (what COVERED verdicts cite):**

| Surface | Location | Size |
|---|---|---|
| Playwright flow specs | `frontend/e2e/flows/01-…20-*.spec.ts` | 20 specs |
| VRT parity | `frontend/e2e/visual/parity.spec.ts` | 1 suite |
| Vitest component/unit | `frontend/__tests__/*.test.{jsx,js}` | 138 files |
| Backend pytest | `tests/*.py` (repo root) | ~100 files |
| Producer-signup smoke | `.github/scripts/staging_smoke.py` + `staging-smoke.yml` | 5-step pipeline |

**Premise corrections vs the ticket text (meta-patterns §1 — surfaced, not silently accepted):**

1. **Backend tests live at repo-root `tests/`, not `backend/tests/`** (`pytest.ini`/CI run `pytest tests/`). New pytest files will be `tests/test_manual_*.py` — same naming, corrected directory.
2. **`testMatch` verified ✓** — `frontend/playwright.config.ts` uses `e2e/flows/**/*.spec.ts`, which matches the planned `e2e/flows/manual/*.spec.ts` with no config change (as the ticket predicted).
3. **E2E backend target:** the suite runs a local `next start` but proxies `/api/*` to the **real Railway staging backend** (MEH-1044 note in `e2e.yml`). Destructive CONVERT verdicts therefore need the local-backend path (`scripts/local-backend.sh`, to be written in the conversion stage) — never staging, per Sapir's 13/07 decision.
4. **Two verdicts beyond the ticket's five** were needed and are proposed for approval here: `KEEP-RUNBOOK` (operational runbooks/process docs that are neither tests nor stale — DR drill, Cloudinary cleanup, Tier protocol, etc.) and `UNCLEAR` (explicitly allowed by the ticket's confidence-calibration clause).

## Verdict summary

| Verdict | Count | Meaning / next step |
|---|---|---|
| CONVERT-PW | 508 | → Playwright spec in `frontend/e2e/flows/manual/` |
| COVERED | 322 | Already automated — file:line evidence in row; section becomes pointer stub |
| STALE | 138 | Surface gone / superseded — **delete on approval** (evidence in row) |
| KEEP-RUNBOOK | 51 | Ops runbook / process doc — stays in doc, not converted |
| DEVICE-ONLY | 26 | Real device/human required — consolidated Tier-3 checklist |
| CONVERT-PYTEST | 23 | → `tests/test_manual_*.py` |
| UNCLEAR | 6 | Needs a human call — flagged per row |
| **Destructive** | **183 of 1,074** | Local backend + ephemeral Postgres ONLY (Sapir 13/07) |

Note on volume: 508 CONVERT-PW items ≠ 506 specs. Items cluster into per-section scenarios; the conversion stage groups each section's items into one spec file with one traceability comment per item ("MANUAL_TESTING § {section} item {n}"). Realistic estimate: ~45–60 spec files.

## Findings — suspected app bugs / live regressions (Ticket B input)

Logged only — **no app code touched** in Ticket A.

| # | Finding | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| F1 | LIKE-wildcard escaping documented in checklist does not exist: `%`/`_` are not escaped in search | `backend/app/services/producer_listing.py:246-247`, `backend/app/routers/search.py:72` — possible regression vs PR #199 |
| F2 | Phone-normalization grep guard violated: `replace(/^0` inline logic in 6 files | incl. `frontend/app/[locale]/admin/outreach/page.jsx` — the exact bug class the guard targets |
| F3 | Main Header vitest suite is `describe.skip` since MEH-729 — MEH-731/643 have zero active regression guard; skipped body still asserts 4 nav links incl. מהשכן (contradicts shipped 3) | `frontend/__tests__/Header.test.jsx:93,101,107` vs `components/Header.jsx:152-154` |
| F4 | `/admin/content` is the only admin page not wired to `useAdminAction` — no double-submit protection (MEH-228 gap) | `frontend/app/[locale]/admin/content/page.js:134-143` |
| F5 | OTP confirm rate limit mismatch: checklist says 5/min, code is 3/min; neither limit has a 429 test | `backend/app/routers/producer_me.py:796` |
| F6 | Dead export: `PENDING_WHATSAPP_COMPANION_COPY` has no consumers — dashboard companion copy never renders (lost feature?) | `frontend/lib/producer-status.js:28` |
| F7 | `/neighbor` removed but no redirect rule exists — `/he/neighbor` likely 404s, checklist claims redirect to `/` | no rule in `middleware.js` / `next.config.js` (UNCLEAR rows) |
| F8 | Experiences admin/host email notifications have zero test coverage | `backend/app/routers/experiences.py:265`, `admin_experiences.py:78,146` |
| F9 | `background-attachment: fixed` deliberately reintroduced — the old "comments-only" parallax grep guard now inverted | `frontend/app/globals.css:311-316` |
| F10 | MEH-328 Test D `didUpgrade` UI branch ("הצטרפת!" vs inbox-check) — the checklist's LOAD-BEARING failure signal — is unasserted anywhere | backend contract covered at `tests/test_api.py:484-505`; frontend branch untested |
| F11 | Doc-structure bugs in MANUAL_TESTING.md: MEH-551 status-table row orphaned at :2215; MAP-16 item mis-homed under MEH-997 (:2273); items :784-788 sit under MEH-1011 heading but belong to MEH-530 | fixed automatically by the conversion rewrite |
| F12 | MEH-1100 nav-guard dialogs have no repeatable test (jsdom limitation documented in test header) — closable via PW `page.on("dialog")` | `frontend/__tests__/EditUnsavedGuard.test.jsx:1-8` |

**Highest-value conversion targets surfaced by triage:** mobile `/map` near-me (`07-gps-button.spec.ts:12` skips mobile entirely); MEH-722 map legend (zero coverage, live feature); `CitySearch` autocomplete (reused 3+ surfaces, stubbed everywhere); static pages `/privacy` `/terms` `/accessibility` (spec 13 covers only `/about`+`/events`); MEH-853 upgrade-path submit body (vitest gap).

## How to read the table

Columns: **Section** (heading verbatim from MANUAL_TESTING.md) · **#** (item ordinal within section) · **Item** (Hebrew verbatim, trimmed) · **Verdict** · **Destructive** (yes = local-backend-only) · **Evidence** (file:line for COVERED/STALE) · **Notes**.
